# Agents · I · part 3

1,008 entries, implementer to inference-engine-expert. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **implementer**: Use for all implementation tasks where the plan is already clear. Trigger when Opus has decided what to build and needs it executed. Examples: implement this plan, write tests for X, refactor Y to follow Z pattern, fix this failing test, generate a config file, write boilerplate, rename variables, apply a pattern across files. Do NOT use when the task requires architectural judgment or when it is unclear what the correct solution is. ([JBSommeling/claude-config](https://github.com/JBSommeling/claude-config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.header.md))
- **implementer**: Autonomous feature implementer for the Ralph loop. Picks the next incomplete PRD feature, implements it, tests it, and commits. Designed to run headless with no user interaction. ([jcquinlan/claude-harness](https://github.com/jcquinlan/claude-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · 2 copies)
- **implementer**: Ejecuta tareas de tasks.md con TDD estricto rojo-verde-refactor, una a una, mostrando la salida real de los tests. ([jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude](https://github.com/jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/implementer.md) · 3 copies)
- **implementer**: Ejecutor TDD del circuito SDD. Toma tareas de tasks.md y las implementa en ciclo rojo-verde-refactor, una a una. Úsalo en /sdd-implement. Delega en los especialistas de dominio cuando la tarea es de su terreno y devuelve el control. ([jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude](https://github.com/jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · 3 copies)
- **implementer**: Ejecutor TDD que implementa tareas aprobadas y coordina especialistas. ([jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude](https://github.com/jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/implementer.md) · 6 copies)
- **implementer**: Ejecuta las tareas de tasks.md con TDD estricto rojo-verde-refactor, una a una, mostrando la salida real de los tests. ([jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude](https://github.com/jechamo/Estructura_inicial_claude/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implementer.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **implementer**: Implements a single task: writes the minimal code that turns the test-writer's failing tests green, or patches forward over independently-confirmed review claims. The factory's `implement` producer stage. ([jfa94/factory](https://github.com/jfa94/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Implementer subagent for one ticket from a spec (mattpocock /implement-spec, /implement) or one task from a written plan. Works in its own worktree on its own branch. Use for well-specified tickets where the spec already defines behavior and seams. Not for architecture, open-ended debugging, or review. ([Jiranon-K/agent-skills](https://github.com/Jiranon-K/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Implementa exatamente o escopo do ticket seguindo o PLAN.md aprovado, sem expandir. Use depois que o plano existir no worktree. ([jjr-dev/orchestrator-orca](https://github.com/jjr-dev/orchestrator-orca/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Implement one task from a plan. Reads project CLAUDE.md, writes production code + happy-path tests, runs the test gate, commits. Use for non-trivial code changes when a plan exists. Always Sonnet. ([jLAM-ERR/autodidact-plugins](https://github.com/jLAM-ERR/autodidact-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/autodidact-workflow/agents/implementer.md))

- **implementer**: Use this agent to execute an already-written plan (from the planner agent, or a short explicit instruction) against this Go API server — general code changes, bug fixes, and feature work, not limited to adding a brand-new CRUD resource (use resource-scaffolder for that specific, template-driven case). It has full read/write tool access, verifies its own work with make vet/make test, and runs one bounded self-review pass over its own diff before reporting done. Examples: ([johnnyc20/myproject](https://github.com/johnnyc20/myproject/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Bounded production implementer. Use PROACTIVELY for well-scoped implementation work after architecture and acceptance criteria are already decided by the orchestrator — feature slices, bug fixes, refactors, configuration and schema edits with explicit boundaries. Makes the smallest coherent change, validates it, and reports back. Never commits, pushes, or deploys. ([jose2505207-eng/fable-orchestration](https://github.com/jose2505207-eng/fable-orchestration/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Use proactively and automatically for every bounded production-code change after the relevant architecture and acceptance criteria are known. Do not wait for the user to request this agent. Implementation agent for bounded, explicitly assigned code changes - reads existing architecture and tests before editing, makes minimal coherent changes, runs targeted validation, and never expands scope on its own. ([jose2505207-eng/open-roots](https://github.com/jose2505207-eng/open-roots/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md))

- **implementer**: Makes the frozen, human-approved automated tests pass and executes the frozen manual tests. - **implementer**: Builds the unit of work — writes, modifies, and fixes your product code to satisfy the approved plan and pass the test oracle. Do NOT use to author or change tests — use test-engineer; do NOT use to review a proposed change — use code-reviewer; do NOT use to diagnose an observed failure — use debugger. - **index**: Antiphon 通过与本地 `antiphond` 守护进程通信的小型适配器来聆听智能体会话。每个适配器都是**故障即放行（fail-open）**的：如果 Antiphon 未安装或未运行，你的智能体的行为与插件不存在时完全一致。会话绝不会因守护进程而阻塞。 ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/zh-Hans/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/index.md) · ★1)
- **index**: Antiphon 透過小巧的轉接器聆聽代理工作階段，這些轉接器與本機的 `antiphond` 常駐程式溝通。每個轉接器都是 **fail-open**：如果沒有安裝或沒有執行 Antiphon，你的代理的行為就跟外掛不存在時一模一樣。工作階段永遠不會因為常駐程式而被卡住。 ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/zh-Hant/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/index.md) · ★1)
- **index**: Antiphon listens to agent sessions through small adapters that talk to the local `antiphond` daemon. Every adapter is **fail-open**: if Antiphon isn't installed or isn't running, your agent behaves exactly as if the plugin didn't exist. Nothing ever blocks a session on the daemon. ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/docs/agents/index.md) · ★1)
- **index**: Antiphon слушает сессии агентов через небольшие адаптеры, которые общаются с локальным демоном `antiphond`. Каждый адаптер — **fail-open**: если Antiphon не установлен или не запущен, агент ведёт себя ровно так, как будто плагина не существует. Ничто и никогда не блокирует сессию из-за демона.
